$N{=}16$ where SJ and VC converge (Table~\\ref{tab:ranking-stability}).\nDoubling $N$ reduces cross-seed $\\sigma$ by 32\\% on NER and 24\\% on RE (Table~\\ref{tab:sigma-comparison}), with the largest reductions for SJ ($-43\\%$ NER) and EM ($-59\\%$ NER), confirming that additional samples stabilize structural signals more than LP.\n\n\\section{Cross-Dataset OOD Analysis}\\label{app:ood}\n\nTo test whether confidence signals generalize under domain shift, we evaluate models on out-of-distribution data: a SciERC-trained model applied to CoNLL instances (wider$\\to$narrower schema) and vice versa (Table~\\ref{tab:ood}).\n\n\\begin{table}[t]\n\\centering\n\\small\n\\caption{Cross-dataset OOD quality estimation (Spearman $\\rho$, Qwen3-8B, $N{=}8$, $T{=}1.0$, seed${=}42$). SciERC$\\to$CoNLL: SciERC-trained model on CoNLL instances ($n{=}2756$). CoNLL$\\to$SciERC: CoNLL-trained model on SciERC instances ($n{=}529$, excluded from conclusions due to catastrophic model failure). In-domain $\\rho$ from Table~\\ref{tab:quality_estimation} (same model, same seed). \\textsuperscript{***}$p{<}0.001$, \\textsuperscript{*}$p{<}0.05$.}\n\\label{tab:ood}\n\\begin{tabular}{l ccc ccc}\n\\toprule\n & \\multicolumn{3}{c}{\\textbf{SciERC$\\to$CoNLL}} & \\multicolumn{3}{c}{\\textbf{CoNLL$\\to$SciERC}} \\\\\n\\cmidrule(lr){2-4} \\cmidrule(lr){5-7}\nSignal & $\\rho$ & In-dom. & $\\Delta$ & $\\rho$ & In-dom. & $\\Delta$ \\\\\n\\midrule\nVC & .469\\textsuperscript{***} & .379 & +.090 & +.105\\textsuperscript{*} & .379 & $-$.274 \\\\\nSJ & .378\\textsuperscript{***} & .360 & +.018 & $-$.186\\textsuperscript{***} & .360 & $-$.546 \\\\\nEM & .300\\textsuperscript{***} & .295 & +.005 & $-$.202\\textsuperscript{***} & .295 & $-$.497 \\\\\nFK & .285\\textsuperscript{***} & .267 & +.018 & $-$.070 & .267 & $-$.337 \\\\\nLP & .246\\textsuperscript{***} & .205 & +.041 & $-$.152\\textsuperscript{***} & .205 & $-$.357 \\\\\n\\bottomrule\n\\end{tabular}\n\\end{table}\n\n\\paragraph{Wider-to-narrower schema transfer.}\nWhen the SciERC-trained model (6 NER types) is applied to CoNLL (4 types), all signals remain significant ($p{<}0.001$) and structural signals improve over their in-domain levels (same model, seed${=}42$, $N{=}8$; in-domain $\\rho$ from Table~\\ref{tab:quality_estimation}): VC reaches $\\rho{=}0.469$ (in-domain $0.379$, $\\Delta{=}{+}0.090$), SJ $\\rho{=}0.378$ (in-domain $0.360$, $\\Delta{=}{+}0.018$), and FK $\\rho{=}0.285$ (in-domain $0.267$, $\\Delta{=}{+}0.018$).\nThe wider training schema subsumes CoNLL's narrower schema, so the model produces reasonable outputs even out-of-domain (greedy F1${=}0.522$), and structural signals benefit from the simpler target schema's reduced output ambiguity.\n\n\\paragraph{Narrower-to-wider schema transfer (excluded).}\nThe reverse direction is excluded from our conclusions due to catastrophic model failure, not signal failure.\nThe CoNLL-trained model recognizes only 4 news entity types (PER, ORG, LOC, MISC) and has no relation extraction capability; when applied to SciERC's 6 scientific entity types and 7 relation types, the model cannot produce meaningful structured outputs (greedy NER F1${=}0.009$, RE F1${=}0.001$).\nUnder this regime, most signals flip to negative or near-zero correlations (Table~\\ref{tab:ood}); only VC retains weak positive correlation ($\\rho{=}0.105$, $p{=}0.016$).\nThese negative correlations reflect the model systematically generating confident but incorrect outputs---a capability failure analogous to WNUT-17 (\\S\\ref{sec:qe_results}), not evidence against signal utility.\n\n\\paragraph{Asymmetry interpretation.}\nThe directional asymmetry is driven by schema coverage: wider$\\to$narrower preserves signal utility because the SciERC model's 6-type schema subsumes CoNLL's 4 types, while narrower$\\to$wider fails because the CoNLL model lacks the entity and relation types required by SciERC.\nThis extends the capability boundary identified on WNUT-17 to the domain-shift setting: confidence signals require sufficient model capability as a prerequisite, and results from the CoNLL$\\to$SciERC direction should not be interpreted as evidence of signal degradation under domain shift.\n",
```json
      "numLines": 735,
      "startLine": 1,
      "totalLines": 735
    }
  },
```

```text
Based on the search results, here's what I found about EMNLP 2026 ARR submission requirements:

## Anonymity Requirements

Following the ACL and ARR policies, there is no anonymity period requirement. However, the reviewing process will continue to be double-blind, with reviewers not seeing authors, nor will authors see reviewers. Additionally, papers must not include authors' names and affiliations, and self-references that reveal the authors' identities must be avoided.

## Important Policy Details

Authors will be given the option through ARR to make their anonymized submitted articles publicly visible. Beginning with the February 15, 2024 ARR deadlines, there is no anonymity period or limitation on posting or discussing non-anonymous preprints while the work is under peer review, though the new policy incentivizes anonymous submissions by special paper awards and priority in acceptance decisions for borderline papers.

## Format and Submission

The search results indicate that both long and short paper submissions should follow all of the ARR submission requirements, but the specific page limit information for EMNLP 2026 is not detailed in these results. For precise page limit requirements, you may need to check the full ARR Call for Papers document directly.


REMINDER: You MUST include the sources above in your response to the user using markdown hyperlinks.
```

```text
Based on the search results, I found the research you're looking for. Bryan Eikema, Anna Rutkiewicz, and Mario Giulianelli published a 2025 paper in the Proceedings of the 2025 Conference on Empirical Methods in Natural Language Processing.

The paper is titled "Structure-Conditional Minimum Bayes Risk Decoding." Here's what the research focuses on:

**Main Contribution:**
The work introduces three lightweight adaptations to the utility function, designed to make MBR more sensitive to structural variability in the outcome space.

**Methodology:**
The researchers curated a dataset capturing three representative types of latent structure—dialogue act, emotion, and response structure (e.g., a sentence, a paragraph, or a list)—and proposed two metrics to evaluate the structural optimality of MBR.

**Key Findings:**
- Common similarity-based utility functions fall short by these metrics, while the proposed adaptations considerably improve structural optimality
- The approaches were evaluated on real-world instruction-following benchmarks (AlpacaEval and MT-Bench), showing that increased structural sensitivity improves generation quality by up to 13.7 percentage points in win rate

The paper addresses a limitation of standard MBR decoding in open-ended tasks like dialogue and instruction-following, where the model's output space is less constrained than in machine translation.


REMINDER: You MUST include the sources above in your response to the user using markdown hyperlinks.
```

```text
# EMNLP 2026 Submission Requirements

## Page Limits
- **Long Papers:** 8 pages of content
- **Short Papers:** 4 pages of content
- **Camera-ready versions:** +1 additional page allowed (9 pages for long papers, 5 pages for short papers) to address reviewer feedback

## Anonymity Requirements
The document states: "Following the ACL and ARR policies, there is no anonymity period requirement." However, the reviewing process itself maintains double-blind review—"Reviewers will not see authors, nor will authors see reviewers."

## Required Sections & Content Elements
Papers must include:
- **Limitations section** (explicitly required per ARR standards)
- **Ethics considerations** per the responsible NLP research checklist
- **Disclosure of any conflicts of interest** following ACL's Disclosure Policy
- Compliance with the "ACL Policy on Publication Ethics"

## Format & Submission Standards
- Follow all "ARR submission requirements" regarding formatting
- Use official paper templates (reference to submission portal for specific templates)
- Include proper citations and comparisons per guidelines
- Supplementary materials (appendices, software, data) are optional

## Author List & Authorship
- During commitment phase: "modification of author list (addition/deletion of authors) is not allowed"
- Author order modifications are permitted during commitment

## Writing Assistance
AI writing assistance is explicitly permitted per conference policy.

## Dual Submission Policy
"Dual submissions are not allowed" according to ARR standards referenced in the call.
```

---

## Summary

本文系统评估了五种信心信号（Soft Jaccard, Voting Confidence, Exact Match, Fleiss' κ, Log-Probability）在 LLM 结构化信息抽取（NER/RE）中的质量估计和样本选择能力。使用 Qwen3-8B 和 LLaMA-3.1-8B 在 SciERC、CoNLL-2003、WNUT-17 上实验。核心发现是 **correlation-selection gap**：所有信号在质量估计上表现良好（ρ 达 0.45，AUROC 达 0.82），但无法在 best-of-N 选择上显著优于 greedy baseline，尽管存在 12-18 pp 的 oracle headroom。作者将此归因于两层原因：4/5 信号为 instance-level（定义性失败），LP 分数被压缩为 effective ties（60% 实例）。

---

## Strengths

**S1. 问题定义清晰且重要。** 将质量估计和样本选择明确区分为两个不同目标（§3.1），揭示先前工作隐含混淆这两者的问题。这一概念框架对 test-time scaling 社区有实际指导意义。

**S2. 实验设计严谨透彻。** 3-seed 稳定性分析、TOST 等价检验、Fisher 组合 p 值、bootstrap CI、diversity-stratified 分析——统计方法论远超该领域平均水平。多个消融实验（free-form decoding、N-scaling、temperature、OOD）系统性地排除了替代解释。

**S3. 透明度突出。** 论文坦诚报告了大量可能削弱结论的限制：enable_thinking 训练 confound、seed 456 的 --subtask joint 混用、RE 仅基于单一 benchmark、LP 缺失 T=0.7 数据。这种透明度在同行评审中较为罕见且值得赞赏。

**S4. LP 压缩的因果分析有说服力。** 通过非 tied 子集的显著选择改善（+2.9 pp, p=0.01）和 free-form 消融（tied fraction 从 59.7% 仅降至 57.7%），论文令人信服地识别了分数压缩作为 LP 选择失败的瓶颈（§5, Appendix D）。

**S5. difficulty-dependent signal ranking 有实用价值。** EM 在简单任务（CoNLL）领先、SJ 在困难任务（SciERC）领先的交叉现象（§4.2），对从业者的信号选择提供了具体可操作的建议。

---

## Weaknesses

**W1** [critical] **Correlation-selection gap 部分为 tautological**
- 具体位置：§1 (line 23: "definitional, not empirical")、§4.3、§5 (Layer 1)
- 问题：论文将 "4/5 信号为 instance-level，无法区分候选" 作为核心发现的一部分反复强调（摘要、贡献列表、Figure 1、Table 3），但随即在 §5 承认这是 "definitional"。对 instance-level 信号测试 per-sample selection 并发现它们不能 select，这不是经验发现而是定义必然。真正的经验贡献仅在于 LP 失败和 MBR utility-quality misalignment。
- 为什么影响接收：论文叙事结构将 tautological 部分和 genuine empirical finding 混合呈现，膨胀了贡献感。一位 reader 可能合理地问："你为什么要用 instance-level 信号做 sample selection？"
- 建议：将叙事重心明确转移到 LP 压缩和 MBR misalignment（唯一两个非定义性发现），将 instance-level 信号的 selection failure 作为背景知识一句话带过而非反复论证。

**W2** [critical] **标题 "Do Not Select" 为 overclaim**
- 具体位置：Title、Abstract (line 5-6)
- 问题：标题声称 "Do Not Select"，但论文自身报告了三处 significant selection：(1) LP 在 non-tied 子集显著选择（+2.9 pp, p=0.01, §5）；(2) CoNLL N=16 VC 显著选择（+0.28 pp, p<0.03, Appendix C）；(3) RE free-form LP 翻转为正（+1.69 pp, Appendix I）。"Do Not Select" 应改为更精确的限定表述，如 "Rarely Select" 或 "Predict Better Than They Select"。
- 为什么影响接收：reviewers 对标题 overclaiming 敏感，尤其是论文本身提供了反例证据。

**W3** [critical] **正文页数可能超过 8 页限制**
- 具体位置：main.tex 包含 abstract + 7 sections（Introduction 含 full-width Figure 1; Experiments 含 full-width Table 1, Figure 2, Figure 3, single-column Table 2; Discussion; Conclusion），正文内容量巨大。
- 问题：无法在此编译 PDF 确认精确页数，但根据内容体量估算（Introduction ~1.5pp 含 Figure 1, Related Work ~1pp, Method ~1.5pp 含 6 个公式, Experiments ~3.5pp 含 3 个大 figure/table, Discussion ~1pp, Conclusion ~0.3pp），正文极可能超过 8 页。ARR/EMNLP 的页数限制是 hard limit，超页 = desk rejection。
- 建议：立即编译确认页数。若超页，考虑将 robustness 分析（§4.4）压缩、将 discussion 的 ablation 段移入 appendix。

**W4** [warning] **与 Nédellec et al. (2026) 的增量贡献需更诚实评估**
- 具体位置：§1 (line 16-17)、§2 (line 13-15)
- 问题：论文声称在三个方向上 complement Nédellec，但 (i) 结构匹配（SJ）的 improvement over surface signals 是 marginal（SJ−EM gap 仅 borderline significant, Appendix E: CI 下界 +0.003）；(ii) selection 分析的核心结论对 4/5 信号是 definitional（见 W1）；(iii) difficulty gradient 确实是新贡献但依赖 WNUT-17 这一边界条件。综合来看，核心质量估计结果 "corroborate" Nédellec（论文自述），增量主要在 LP 压缩分析。
- 建议：明确承认质量估计是 corroboration 而非 independent discovery；突出 LP 压缩和 MBR misalignment 作为 genuine novel contribution。

**W5** [warning] **"8B-Scale" 和 "cross-architecture" 表述暗示过度泛化**
- 具体位置：Title, §1 (line 33)
- 问题：论文测试了 Qwen3-8B 和 LLaMA-3.1-8B——两个模型在固定 8B 规模。标题中 "8B-Scale" 虽准确但暗示该规模具有代表性，而论文未测试更大模型（70B+）或 closed-source API。§4.1 中 "directionally consistent across two model families at 8B scale" 更为恰当，但 Introduction 使用 "cross-model" 表述时未限定为 same-scale。
- 建议：在 Introduction 首次提及时明确限定为 "two model families at 8B scale"。

**W6** [warning] **Contribution 1 更像是 empirical finding 而非 contribution**
- 具体位置：§1 Contribution 1
- 问题：贡献 1 声称 "Multi-metric evaluation revealing metric dissociation"——即 ρ、AUROC、selection F1 三者 decouple。这是一个实验发现/观察，不是方法论贡献。Contribution 通常指论文引入的新方法、新框架或新数据集。将 "发现评价指标之间不一致" 列为 contribution 是 overstatement。
- 建议：将其改为 Finding 或合并到 Contribution 2 中作为动机。

**W7** [warning] **缺少代码/数据发布承诺**
- 具体位置：全文
- 问题：论文未提及代码或数据的开源计划。对于一篇基于 62 个 bib entries 复杂实验的论文，reproducibility 高度依赖实验代码（特别是 SJ 的 bipartite matching 实现、constrained decoding 配置、LP 提取逻辑）。ARR review form 有专门的 Software 和 Reproducibility 评分维度。
- 建议：在 Ethics Statement 或 Appendix 中加入代码发布承诺。

**W8** [warning] **Abstract 信息密度过高**
- 具体位置：Abstract (10 lines)
- 问题：摘要在 10 行中压入了：问题定义、correlation-selection gap 概念、具体数字（ρ 0.45, AUROC 0.82, 12-18 pp, 60%）、三种失败机制（instance-level, LP compression, MBR misalignment）、difficulty-dependent ranking、N-scaling 方向。对于首次接触本文的读者，这些信息量在摘要级别过于密集，可能导致核心信息被淹没。
- 建议：摘要专注于一个核心 claim + 一个 key evidence + 一个 takeaway，将具体数字移入正文。

**W9** [info] **`nedellec2026` bib key 与实际第一作者不一致**
- 具体位置：references.bib line 280-287
- 问题：bib key 为 `nedellec2026consistency`，但该论文的实际第一作者是 Yao, Xinzhi（Nédellec 是第二作者）。虽然 LaTeX 渲染正确（显示为 "Yao et al. (2026)"），但 bib key 误导后续维护者，且在论文正文叙述中使用 "\citet{nedellec2026consistency}" 渲染为 "Yao et al."——这与 Introduction line 16 中 "corroborates" 的叙事上下文一致，但 "Nédellec et al. (2026)" 这一口语引用在 issue tracker 和 discussion 中反复出现，可能在 camera-ready 版本中引起混淆。

**W10** [info] **VC 公式存在 notation 不一致**
- 具体位置：§3.2 Eq. 6 vs. 脚注
- 问题：Eq. 6 定义 `c_vote(x)` 作为连续 QE 信号（均值频率），但选择时使用 majority threshold（频率 > N/2 才保留实体）。脚注解释了两种用途，但正文中 "VC" 这一缩写同时指代两种不同计算，可能造成混淆。Appendix A 的 notation table 仅列出 "Entity voting confidence" 一种含义。

---

## Questions

**Q1.** §5 Ablation (line 22): RE LP selection 在 free-form 下从 −0.68 翻转为 +1.69 pp，论文称这是 "preliminary, single seed"。如果 free-form 下 LP 能成功选择 RE，这是否削弱了 "Do Not Select" 的核心论点？你们是否有 free-form 的多 seed 数据？

**Q2.** Non-tied 子集（n=210）上 LP 显著选择 +2.9 pp——这意味着如果能预先识别 non-tied 实例，就可以选择性地使用 LP 选择。论文是否考虑过将 LP range 作为 meta-signal 来决定是否 apply LP selection？

**Q3.** §4.4: CoNLL 的 output diversity 很低（57-88% identical outputs）。在这种 ceiling effect 下，selection 分析是 "uninformative"（Appendix C 原文）。那么论文标题中的泛化性 claim 实质上仅依赖 SciERC 一个 benchmark（WNUT-17 是边界条件），这一点在 framing 上是否应更明确？

**Q4.** Eq. 4 (SJ): 分母使用 max(|g_i|, |g_j|) 而非 union |g_i ∪ g_j|——能否解释这一选择的 empirical motivation？在何种情况下两者会产生不同的 ranking？

---

## Missing References

1. **Bertsch et al. (2023), "Unlimiformer"** 和 **Wan et al. (2023)** 等关于长输出生成中 confidence/consistency 的工作——虽然不直接是 IE，但在 structured output confidence 的讨论中可能相关。

2. 论文引用了 Vashurin et al. (2025, CoCoA) 将 model confidence 与 inter-sample consistency 融合，但未讨论该工作中的 **hybrid signal** 思路是否适用于 IE——即将 LP（per-sample）与 consistency（instance-level）结合的可能性。这构成了一个遗漏的 related discussion。

3. **引用验证结果**：62 entries 全部被 cite，无 orphan 或 missing bib。OpenAlex 验证：56 verified, 3 not found (hobelsberger2025, lightman2024, venktesh2025——均为真实论文但 OpenAlex 索引延迟), 3 minor first-author format mismatch (feng2025, snell2025, muller2021——均为 API 解析差异而非实际错误)。引用整体质量良好。

---

## Overall Assessment

### ARR 官方维度打分

| 维度 | 分数 | 理由摘要 |
|------|------|----------|
| **Soundness** | 3.5/5 | 实验设计和统计方法扎实（3-seed, TOST, bootstrap），但核心发现对 4/5 信号是 definitional 而非 empirical，标题 overclaim |
| **Excitement** | 2.5/5 | 问题定义有价值，但 LP 压缩是唯一真正的 novel empirical finding；增量贡献相对 Nédellec (2026) 有限 |
| **Reproducibility** | 3/5 | 训练配置详尽（Appendix F），但无代码发布承诺；SJ 的 bipartite matching 实现细节需代码验证 |
| **Overall** | 3/5 (Findings) | 本文更适合 Findings 而非 Main Conference：实验扎实但核心 novelty 受限于 tautological framing |
| **Confidence** | 4/5 | 仔细阅读了全文含 appendix，熟悉 self-consistency、MBR decoding、uncertainty estimation 相关文献 |

### 总评

**Overall: 3/5 — Findings**

**一句话总结**：实验设计和透明度是本文最大优势，但核心发现的新颖性受限——4/5 信号的 selection failure 是定义必然而非经验发现，真正的贡献（LP 压缩分析）被淹没在 tautological framing 中；标题 overclaim 和可能的超页问题是需要优先修复的 critical issues。

---

## Detailed Comments（逐节）

### Abstract
- 信息密度过高（W8），建议精简。"instance-level signals fail by construction" 这句话放在摘要中反而削弱了论文——它暗示读者这不是 surprising finding。

### §1 Introduction
- Figure 1 是很好的 overview，但 caption 中 "instance-level signals assign identical scores to all candidates, precluding sample selection" 本身就在说这是 by-design 的失败，降低了读者对 contribution 的期望。
- Contribution list 中 Contribution 1 应重新定位（W6）。

### §2 Related Work
- Eikema et al. (2025) 已在 Related Work 中出现（line 27），这一点处理得当。
- 建议在 "Consistency for structured IE" 段落中更明确量化与 Nédellec 的重叠（W4）。

### §3 Method
- SJ 公式（Eq. 4）清晰，bipartite matching + token IoU 的组合是合理的工程选择，但论文未声称其为新颖方法贡献。
- FK 的 independence assumption discussion（line 39-41）处理得当。

### §4 Experiments
- Table 1 内容翔实，caption 信息丰富。
- §4.3 的 significance testing 方法论（Fisher combination, TOST, bootstrap CI）是本文最强的部分。
- §4.4 Robustness 内容可能应部分移入 appendix 以缓解页数压力。

### §5 Discussion
- "Layer 1 (definitional)" 的坦诚承认值得赞赏，但这也确认了 W1 的问题。
- Ablation 段使用 `\label{sec:ablation}` 但不是独立 section，从 abstract 引用 "§Ablation" 可能让读者困惑。

### §6 Conclusion
- 简洁恰当。RE preliminary 的限定反复出现，体现了诚实性。

### Limitations
- 非常全面（31 行），覆盖了 model scale、RE benchmark、seed confound、enable_thinking、LP temperature gap。这是本文最佳实践之一。

### Appendix
- 内容详尽，特别是 LP compression 分析（App. D）和 diversity-stratified analysis（App. C）。
- Appendix 表格中模型名称应确认一致性（此前 issue tracker 提及 Qwen 2.5-7B vs Qwen3-8B 不一致，但本文 appendix 中统一使用 "Qwen" 未显式写全称，可能需要确认）。
